Story 1.5 substrate-tier-1-and-tier-2-landed ledger-status).

**Substrate-vs-substantive split — deferred legs:** Per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]` — Story 1.5 engineering substrate Closed by [edit] on Tasks 1-7 + local CI gates green (Story 1.5 is engineering with direct objective evidence on substrate landing); ADR-0006 trustee-ratification leg + live Cloud KMS provisioning leg D1-1.5 (analogous to Story 1.2 D1-1.2) + KEK rotation execution leg D2-1.5 (cadence-driven 12-month) + DEK migration saga substantive wiring leg D3-1.5 (Story 1.10+ trigger) + high-sensitivity-tier IAM project topology leg D4-1.5 (Story 1.15 trigger) + KEK-roots destruction approval workflow leg D5-1.5 (operations-policy territory) + ADR-0006 trustee-ratification leg D6-1.5 (light-touch ratification path; security-review checklist gates the flip) + substantive PII-bearing column landings per per-Epic Story leg D7-1.5 (Stories 3.1+/3.3+/3.5+/6.x/9.x) + FR-74 PII shielding CI gate leg D8-1.5 (Story 1.16b trigger) + per-Pariwar HMAC key derivation policy substantive choice leg D9-1.5 (Story 1.6 trigger) + audit-log entry on every encrypt/decrypt op leg D10-1.5 (Story 1.10 KmsProvider.auditHook seam) + branded PariwarId substituted into EncryptionContext leg D11-1.5 (Story 1.7 trigger) + object storage tier policy ADR co-trigger leg D12-1.5 (Story 9.2 closure) + AAD canonical-JSON consolidation leg D13-1.5 (deviation-derived; Story 1.10+ consolidation trigger) + auto-encrypt-on-write inside Drizzle customType leg D14-1.5 (deviation-derived; Drizzle 0.46+ async customType OR Story 1.9+ trigger) = Resolved via explicit deferral with rationale enumerated in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` D1-1.5 through D14-1.5.

**Architecture-vs-epic-AC divergence reaffirmation:** Per `[[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]]` — architecture commits properties (workspace location is architectural property territory); ADRs commit cloud controls; PRD/epics commit policy. Epic AC line 1071 ("the encryption envelope is authored in `packages/crypto`") is loose phrasing parallel to the Story 1.2 epic AC's "packages/db" + the Story 1.1 epic AC's "apps/member" — all authored before architecture's substantive `packages/domain/` consolidation (architecture line 4341-4360 + 4539) was canonical. The Story 1.5 resolution mirrors the Story 1.2 resolution: architecture wins; `packages/crypto` was never created; substrate substantively populates `packages/domain/src/encryption/` per architecture §Cross-cutting concerns line 4539. **Story 1.5 does NOT create a new `packages/crypto` workspace.** Documented in Dev Notes + this Decision.

**Tink-TypeScript sunset resolution:** Architecture §2.7 line 1508 commits "Library: Google Tink (committed in an ADR alongside Cloud KMS integration)." At architecture-authoring time (Step 4 in 2026-05) Tink was Google's recommended cross-language crypto library. The empirical reality at Story 1.5 execution time (2026-06): Tink-Java + Tink-Go + Tink-Python + Tink-C++ + Tink-Obj-C remain actively maintained; **Tink-TypeScript was deprecated in 2023 + sunset in 2024 + last npm published in 2022.** The architectural commitment to "Google Tink" is preserved by: (a) implementing the **Tink envelope shape** (per-row DEK + KEK-wrapped DEK + AEAD ciphertext + auth tag + AAD binding); (b) using the **Tink-recommended algorithms** (AES-256-GCM for AEAD; HMAC-SHA-256 for MAC); (c) using **Cloud KMS native operations** (EncryptDecrypt + MacSign) for KEK + HMAC ops — same primitives Tink wraps; (d) using **Node `crypto`** for AES-256-GCM AEAD + HMAC-SHA-256 — Node `crypto` is the canonical Node-runtime primitive surface; same algorithm choices Tink would use. ADR-0006 substantively records this; the architectural property holds; the library implementation adapts to the empirical TS-ecosystem reality.

**Ratifying trustees:** _pending Trustee Panel session for ADR-0004 ratification leg_; the substrate-author-commit leg is engineering closure with objective evidence + does not require independent trustee ratification per the Story 1.2 closure-language precedent.

**Context.** Story 1.3 is the third Epic 1 engineering story (`[PRIMITIVE]`) — the events package substrate for §1.14 (event history is source-of-truth; persisted state is optimization only) + AR-8 (`packages/events` immutability) + AR-14 (member state machine consuming events) + AR-57 (determinism & replay) + UX-DR74 (Account State Machine framework primitive). It commits the **generic** event log + state-machine substrate that every downstream Story consumes (Story 3.1+ member lifecycle, Story 6.x claim state, Story 7.x pool state, Story 8.x alert state, Story 9.x reconciliation events). Substantive per-domain event types are downstream-Story territory.

Three pre-execution user choices were captured at dev-story Step 1 task_check:

- **Branch strategy.** Stack on `story-1.2-cloud-sql-drizzle` selected (over branching from `main` post-Story-1.2-merge). `story-1.3-events-log-primitive` branched from HEAD `b02d85b` (Story 1.2 review-completed-pre-merge state). Stacked-PR pattern: Story 1.3 PR depends on Story 1.2 PR merging first; mirrors the Story 1.2 stack-on-1.1 pattern.
- **Live-DB integration-test isolation.** Per-test transaction-rollback (Option (a)) selected over per-test schema drop-and-recreate (Option (b)). Each vitest test acquires its own `pg` client from a shared pool, opens a `BEGIN`, runs against a drizzle handle bound to that transaction, and `ROLLBACK`s in `afterEach`. This is the only viable cleanup strategy because the new append-only triggers block `DELETE` / `TRUNCATE` — transaction-rollback is the integration-test substrate that the triggers themselves require.
- **ADR-0004-canonical-json timing.** Author at Story 1.3 closure (over deferring to Story 1.10 audit-log substrate). Rationale: canonical-JSON choice gates the events package replay determinism + is consumed by Stories 1.10 (audit log hash-chain) + 7.x (Pool Engine snapshot). The choice deserves a substantive ADR at first-consumer landing; mirrors Story 1.2 ADR-0003 precedent.

**Decision body:**

1. **`events_log` Drizzle table** authored at `packages/domain/src/schema/events_log.ts` per Task 1 — 8 columns (`event_id` UUID PK with `gen_random_uuid()` default; `stream_id` UUID NOT NULL; `event_type` TEXT NOT NULL with dotted resource.action convention per architecture line 3830-3833; `payload` JSONB NOT NULL; `event_version` BIGINT NOT NULL with `mode: 'number'` per Drizzle pinning + CHECK constraint `>= 1`; `occurred_at` TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT `now()` per architecture §1.11; `actor_id` UUID NULL with `null = system/SIE` per architecture §1.14 line 1262-1268; `pariwar_id` UUID NOT NULL multi-tenant scoping per architecture §1.2 + Story 1.6 RLS-ready). Indexes: `UNIQUE (stream_id, event_version)` for structural optimistic-concurrency enforcement; `(pariwar_id, stream_id, event_version)` for per-tenant per-stream replay loads; `(pariwar_id, occurred_at)` for per-tenant tail reads.

2. **Migration 0001 (`migrations/0001_events-log.sql`)** generated via `drizzle-kit generate --name events-log` then hand-supplemented per Task 1.5 with three append-only Postgres triggers per AR-8 + architecture line 428-431 — `events_log_no_update` BEFORE UPDATE FOR EACH ROW + `events_log_no_delete` BEFORE DELETE FOR EACH ROW + `events_log_no_truncate` BEFORE TRUNCATE — each invoking `events_log_reject_mutation()` plpgsql function which `RAISE EXCEPTION 'events_log is append-only — corrections emit a new event (AR-8)' USING ERRCODE = 'integrity_constraint_violation'`. Migration file header annotated `⚠ DO NOT REGENERATE` mirroring Story 1.2 migration 0000 hand-patch convention. Per-migration atomicity per architecture §1.8 line 1003-1005 means a failed trigger creation rolls back the table creation; idempotency invariant preserved.

3. **Story 1.2 metadata-snapshot drift retrospective discharge** — Story 1.2's third-pass code review removed `pgSchema('drizzle')` from `_baseline.ts` but did not update `migrations/meta/0000_snapshot.json` to drop the `"schemas": { "drizzle": "drizzle" }` reference. Result: Story 1.3's `drizzle-kit generate` initially emitted a spurious `DROP SCHEMA "drizzle"` in migration 0001 (drizzle-kit diffing the snapshots saw the schema as needing-drop). Discharged at Story 1.3 by patching `migrations/meta/0000_snapshot.json` to `"schemas": {}` — bringing the snapshot in line with the current `_baseline.ts` source-of-truth. Future migrations will not re-emit the spurious DROP. Migration 0000 SQL itself (which still contains `CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS "drizzle"`) is unchanged — the drizzle metadata schema continues to be created by the migrator regardless.

4. **`packages/events/` substantive populate** per Task 2-4 — replaced the Story 1.1 `export {}` placeholder at `src/index.ts` with substantive content + added `@twt/domain`, `drizzle-orm ^0.45`, `zod ^3.23` direct dependencies. Files: `src/events-log.ts` (`appendEvent(db, input): Promise<EventRow>` API with `ConcurrencyError` typed-return per architecture §Process patterns line 3873-3884, `loadEvents(db, streamId, { fromVersion?, toVersion? })`, `replayState<S, E>(db, streamId, reducer, initialState): Promise<S>` deterministic fold per architecture §Cross-Cutting #4 + line 1229-1234, defense-in-depth `payloadSchema` Zod-validator parameter, defense-in-depth expectedVersion `>= 0` guard, drizzle-error unwrap helper `extractPgError(err.cause)` to surface the pg SQLSTATE code through drizzle's `Error('Failed query: …')` wrap layer), `src/state-machine.ts` (generic `StateMachine<S extends string, E extends { type: string }>` class + `defineStateMachine` factory + optional documentation-only `transitions[]` table per UX-DR74), `src/canonical-json.ts` (RFC 8785 JCS subset; ~30 lines; lexicographic key ordering via `Object.keys(value).sort()` matching UTF-16 code-unit order per RFC 8785 §3.2.3; NaN/Infinity throw per RFC 8259; `-0` normalizes to `0`), `src/registry.ts` (typed `EVENT_TYPE_REGISTRY` placeholder shape per architecture line 4418 FM-PS-10; substantive event-type entries deferred to downstream Stories), `src/index.ts` (substantive re-exports).

5. **`packages/events/` test surfaces** per Task 5 — 6 vitest files / 31 tests total. Unit tests (no DB; always run): `tests/smoke.test.ts` (preserved Story 1.1 placeholder; 1 test), `tests/state-machine.test.ts` (7 tests: fold over empty stream / one event / multi-event idempotence + step + deterministic equivalence + transitions getter + initial getter), `tests/canonical-json.test.ts` (10 tests: key-order independence + nested key-order + array-order preserved + RFC 8259 string-escaping + `-0` normalization + NaN/Infinity throws + standard JSON literals + round-trip equivalence + deeply-nested-cross-key-order). Live-DB integration tests (SKIP via `describe.skipIf(!DATABASE_URL)` when `DATABASE_URL` unset; 13 tests): `tests/append-event.test.ts` (happy path + sequential append + duplicate-version-raises-ConcurrencyError + negative-expectedVersion rejection + Zod schemaCheck fails-before-INSERT + idempotent re-append PK violation), `tests/replay-state.test.ts` (empty-stream returns initial + deterministic replay + order-sensitive reducer + loadEvents slicing), `tests/append-only.test.ts` (UPDATE/DELETE/TRUNCATE rejection via trigger). `tests/integration-setup.ts` provides per-test transaction-rollback isolation (Task 5.6 Option (a)); savepoint pattern wraps unique-violation tests so the test-isolation transaction isn't poisoned. `tests/README.md` documents the local Docker Postgres 16 invocation pattern.

6. **ConcurrencyError API simplification** — the story-body schema specified `ConcurrencyError(streamId, expectedVersion, currentVersion)`; implementation drops the `currentVersion` field. Rationale: looking up `MAX(event_version)` inside the same transaction that just hit the unique-violation requires savepoints (the violation aborts the surrounding transaction in pg); coupling the error path to transaction-state assumptions we can't make at this layer is fragile. Callers that need the current stream version for retry logic call `loadEvents(db, streamId)` and inspect the last row — which is the same query the error-path would have done, made explicit and called by the caller who understands their own transaction context.

7. **ADR-0004-canonical-json drafted** at `docs/adr/ADR-0004-canonical-json.md` per Task 6.4 — substantive 9-section ADR covering: (a) RFC 8785 JSON Canonicalization Scheme adoption with the 7 rules transcribed (object key sort + array order + RFC 8259 string escaping + RFC 8785 §3.2.2 numeric representation + `-0` normalization + no-whitespace + non-finite throws); (b) hand-rolled ~30-line subset implementation rationale (in-tree code preferred over `canonicalize` npm package transitive-dep cost at v1; subset bounded by v1 payload shapes — paise integers + ISO timestamp strings + UUIDs + booleans + nulls + nested objects); (c) alternatives considered (`canonicalize`, `json-stable-stringify`, `fast-json-stable-stringify`) with rationale for each rejection; (d) constraints (floats > 15 sig digits, BigInt, surrogate pairs out-of-scope at v1); (e) forward path commitment (swap to `canonicalize` npm if subset bites; API does not change across swap; property-test against RFC 8785 reference vectors as the swap-gate); (f) authoring location at `packages/events/src/canonical-json.ts` because the first hash producer is the events package itself; (g) Status `drafted` at Story 1.3 commit → `under-trustee-review` post-Story-1.3-review → `ratified` per Trustee Panel session; (h) closure-language-precision posture per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`. `adr-index.md` Section A row 55 inserted as `drafted`; Status row-count table updated `drafted` 1 → 2, total 125 → 126.

8. **Cross-Story discharge triggers fired by Story 1.3 closure:**
   - **Story 1.5** (Cloud KMS HSM + Tink envelope encryption per AR-12) — Story 1.5 may emit `member.pii_encrypted` events to events_log; the `appendEvent` API surface is consumer-ready.
   - **Story 1.6** (`pariwar_id` first-class + RLS adversarial test per AR-3) — Story 1.6 attaches `pgPolicy` to the `events_log` table (Story 1.3 commits the `pariwar_id` column structurally) + extends the cross-Pariwar adversarial test to assert no event leakage; events_log RLS is Story 1.6 territory.
   - **Story 1.10** (Tamper-evident audit log + hash chain per FR-47 + AR-9/10) — Story 1.10 consumes `canonicalJsonStringify` for `prev_audit_hash` + `audit_hash` computation; the `audit_log_entries` table is a SEPARATE table from `events_log` per architecture §1.5.
   - **Story 1.11a** (audit-log integrity-verification primitive) — consumes `canonicalJsonStringify` for chain verification.
   - **Story 3.1+** (member lifecycle) — substantively authors `packages/domain/src/member/state.ts` per architecture §1.14 line 1227; defines the concrete member-state machine consuming Story 1.3's `StateMachine<S, E>` interface; emits `member.signup_initiated`, `member.kyc_completed`, etc. via `appendEvent`.
   - **Story 6.x** (claim lifecycle) — claim state machine emits `claim.filed`, `claim.verified`, `claim.approved`, `claim.settled`; state derived from event replay per architecture §1.9 line 1019-1031.
   - **Story 7.x** (Pool Engine) — pool state machine emits `pool.spawned`, `pool.frozen`; Pool Engine snapshot writers consume `canonicalJsonStringify` for snapshot integrity hash per architecture §1.6 line 904-934.
   - **Story 8.x** (Alert lifecycle) — alert state machine emits `alert.created`, `alert.dispatched`; state derived from event replay.
   - **Story 9.x** (Reconciliation) — contribution + reconciliation emit `contribution.matched`, `contribution.confirmed`; the monotonic forward chain commitment per epics line 3166 is Story 9.x's concrete exercise of Story 1.3's append-only trigger guarantee.

9. **Closure-language-precision posture per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`:**
   - **Framework + engineering legs** = **Closed by [edit]** on Tasks 1-7 closure + local CI gates green + local Docker Postgres 16 integration tests green. Direct objective evidence: `events_log` table + 3 triggers structurally exist (verified via `psql` `\dft+` + `pg_trigger` query); `pnpm db:migrate` exit 0 with idempotency verified (`drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` row count = 2 after two invocations); `pnpm db:check` exit 0; `pnpm --filter @twt/events test` exit 0 with 31 tests passing across 6 files (18 unit always-on; 13 integration tests against live Docker Postgres 16); `pnpm turbo run lint typecheck test build` zero-regression vs Story 1.2 baseline.
   - **ADR-0004 trustee-ratification leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** pending Trustee Panel session. ADR-0004 Status flips `drafted` → `under-trustee-review` post-Story-1.3 review; ratified status awaits trustee panel.
   - **Live-DB CI substrate leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** per Story 1.6 ownership. Story 1.3 verifies live-DB integration tests locally; Story 1.6 authors the Postgres service-container CI job (the same substrate also gates the cross-Pariwar RLS adversarial test, so it lands once at Story 1.6 and serves both Stories).
   - **Substantive per-domain event-type Zod schemas leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** per downstream-Story ownership discipline. Story 1.3 commits the registry SHAPE; per-domain entries land with each owning Story (member.* at 3.1+, claim.* at 6.x, pool.* at 7.x, alert.* at 8.x, contribution.* at 9.x, audit.* at 1.10).
   - **Substantive Account State composition workload leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** per architecture §Gap Analysis line 4802-4815. Story 1.3 commits the `StateMachine<S, E>` interface; full Account State enumeration (`claim-filed-frozen`, `disbursed-frozen-readable`, `disabled-T+90`, `public-record-∞` end states) is a focused follow-up architectural workload not gated on a single Story.

**Open follow-ups:**

- **Push branch `story-1.3-events-log-primitive` + open PR (stacked on Story 1.2 PR)** + watch CI for `db-check` + `lint` + `typecheck` + `test` + `build` jobs green. Live-DB integration tests do NOT run in CI at Story 1.3 (Story 1.6 substrate).
- **ADR-0004 Trustee Panel review session** scheduling — present the drafted ADR; record ratification or revise-and-resubmit per [[feedback_architecture_vs_adr_boundary]].
- **`packages/domain/migrations/meta/0000_snapshot.json` retrospective patch** documented under Decision body §3 — landed as part of Story 1.3 to discharge the Story 1.2 incomplete `_baseline.ts` cleanup. Optional follow-up: a Story 1.2 retrospective note in deferred-work.md or in this Decision suffices.
- **Substantive event-type Zod schemas** authored per downstream Story discipline — track per `deferred-work.md` D3-1.3.
- **Substantive member-state machine** at `packages/domain/src/member/state.ts` — Story 3.1+ landing per architecture §1.14 line 1227 canonical home commitment (D4-1.3).
- **Substantive Account State composition workload** per architecture §Gap Analysis line 4802-4815 (D5-1.3) — focused follow-up architectural workload; not gated on a single Story.

### Decision 2026-06-08-038: Story 1.2 substantive author-commit — Cloud SQL Postgres + Drizzle migration tooling substrate landed (Terraform IaC for `twt-dev-postgres` + `packages/domain/` Drizzle scaffolding + Secret Manager connection-resolution wiring + ADR-0003 drafted + db-check CI job); `packages/db` → `packages/domain/` workspace-naming divergence resolved per architecture canonical; ADR-0003-datastore-engine slot at `adr-index.md` line 54 flipped `slot-reserved-pre-write` → `drafted`; substantive live-GCP provisioning + envelope-sealing deferred to follow-up per substrate-only execution choice.

**Decision type:** Story 1.2 — Cloud SQL Postgres + Drizzle migration tooling — substrate author-commit.

**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed 2026-06-08; engineering substrate with direct objective evidence (instance configuration committed to repo; `pnpm db:migrate` idempotency proven against local Postgres 16; `pnpm db:check` zero-drift verified; CI workflow updated). Trustee-ratification leg for ADR-0003 = Resolved via explicit deferral pending Trustee Panel session per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), discharging Story 1.2 Tasks 1-7 substrate per the bmad-dev-story workflow.

**Ratifying trustees:** _pending Trustee Panel session for ADR-0003 ratification leg_; the substrate-author-commit leg is engineering closure with objective evidence + does not require independent trustee ratification per the Story 1.2 closure-language posture.

**Context.** Story 1.2 is the second Epic 1 engineering story (`[PRIMITIVE]`) — the substrate half of PR-2 territory per architecture §Implementation Handoff lines 5079-5099. Story 1.1 closed the monorepo bootstrap (Decision 2026-06-08 sprint-status `1-1: done`); Story 1.2 now lands the Cloud SQL Postgres instance + Drizzle ORM + drizzle-kit migration toolchain that every data-model story downstream (1.3 events / 1.5 encryption / 1.6 RLS / 1.7 Pariwar-Passport / 1.10 audit log / 1.12 pg-boss / 3.x members / 4.x rules / 7.x pools / 9.x reconciliation) depends on.

Three pre-execution user choices were captured at dev-story Step 1 task_check:

- **Task 1.1 provisioning strategy.** Option (a) Terraform IaC at `infra/gcp/cloud-sql-dev.tf` selected (over Option (b) gcloud runbook). Rationale: Story 1.15 Dokploy + multi-Pariwar provisioning will reuse this module; Terraform's idempotency + state management pays back from the 2nd Pariwar onward; the `environment` + `availability_type` + `tier` tfvar parameterization makes the same module work for dev / staging / prod with no HCL changes.
- **Live GCP execution scope.** "Substrate + local Postgres verify" selected. The substrate (IaC + Drizzle scaffolding + Secret Manager wiring + ADR-0003 + Decision-log + docs) is fully authored + verified; live `twt-dev-postgres` provisioning against the BigDev-funded `twt-dev` GCP project + the Secret Manager secret-value population are deferred to a follow-up execution. Local Docker Postgres 16 stands in for the live DB to verify `pnpm db:migrate` idempotency + `pnpm db:check` zero-drift at Story 1.2 closure. The deferred work is enumerated in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` "## Story 1.2 deferred" section.
- **Branch strategy.** Stack on `story-1.1-bootstrap` selected. `story-1.2-cloud-sql-drizzle` branched from HEAD `88e41c0` (Story 1.1 review-completed-pre-merge state). Stacked-PR pattern: Story 1.2 PR depends on Story 1.1 PR merging first; rebase if Story 1.1 review surfaces post-merge changes.

**Decision body:**

1. **Terraform IaC for the dev Cloud SQL Postgres instance** authored at `infra/gcp/` per Task 1 — split into focused files (`versions.tf`, `variables.tf`, `network.tf`, `cloud-sql-dev.tf`, `outputs.tf`, `terraform.tfvars.example`, `.gitignore`). Module commits: Postgres 16, asia-south1, ZONAL at dev (REGIONAL at staging+prod via `availability_type` tfvar), `db-custom-2-7680` (2 vCPU / 7.5 GB RAM minimum-viable), `deletion_protection = true`, `backup_configuration` with daily backups + PITR (transaction_log_retention_days = 7; backup_start_time = 20:30 UTC / 02:00 IST), private IP only via Private Services Access (no public IPv4 per architecture §5.8 line 3270), `require_ssl = true`, `cloudsql.enable_pgaudit = on`. Resources: `google_sql_database_instance.main`, `google_sql_database.app` (`twt_dev`), `google_sql_user.app` (`twt_dev_app`, non-superuser, no BYPASSRLS preparing Story 1.6 RLS isolation per architecture §1.2 line 717-725), `google_secret_manager_secret.conn_string` (`twt-dev-cloud-sql-conn-string`), `google_secret_manager_secret_version.conn_string_v1` (postgresql:// URL assembled from random_password + instance private IP). Module parameterized for Story 1.15 reuse (staging + prod via tfvars override).

2. **`packages/domain/` Drizzle scaffolding** authored per Task 2 — `drizzle-orm ^0.45` + `drizzle-kit ^0.31` + `pg ^8.13` + `@types/pg` + `@google-cloud/secret-manager ^6.1` + `dotenv` + `tsx` dependencies (version pins reflect latest stable as of 2026-06; story-file pin guidance `^0.36` / `^0.27` superseded by registry-current versions per "verify against npm registry at dev-time and pin to the matching stable line"). Files: `drizzle.config.ts` (postgresql dialect + `schema './src/schema/*.ts'` + `out './migrations'` + strict+verbose + `__drizzle_migrations` table in `drizzle` schema), `src/db.ts` (createDb factory bound to node-postgres pool; per-workspace pool isolation per architecture §1.1 line 706-710 with default `max: 10` placeholder), `src/secrets.ts` (`resolveConnectionString` + `fetchConnectionString` — Secret Manager via Application Default Credentials when `NODE_ENV === 'production'` OR `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` set OR `DRIZZLE_FORCE_SECRET_MANAGER === '1'`; `DATABASE_URL` env-var fallback otherwise; never logs the value), `src/schema/{index,_baseline}.ts` (schema barrel + migration-zero marker declaring the `drizzle` metadata schema), eight placeholder sub-directories under `src/` per architecture §Complete project directory structure line 4348-4360 with `.gitkeep` + landing-Story README pointers (`policies/` Story 1.6 RLS, `ids/` Story 1.7+ branded types, `encryption/` Story 1.5 column transformers, `snapshot-fixtures/` + `snapshot-adapters/` Story 7.x Pool Engine, `cross-tenant/` Story 1.6 helper, `bank-statement/` Story 9.2, `per-pariwar/bihar/` Story 1.7 + 10.12), `seed/{dev,staging}/` per-environment placeholder dirs with READMEs citing architecture §5.5 "No production PII in dev / staging", `scripts/migrate.ts` (db:migrate wrapper: `resolveConnectionString` → `drizzle-orm/node-postgres/migrator` — required because drizzle-kit's CLI cannot await async credential lookup), `migrations/0000_init-baseline.sql` (drizzle-kit emit, manually patched to `CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS "drizzle"` because drizzle-kit's migrator auto-creates the schema before applying migration 0000), `.env.example` (DATABASE_URL + GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT + GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS + DRIZZLE_LOG_QUERIES + DRIZZLE_FORCE_SECRET_MANAGER), `tests/db.test.ts` (3-case unit test of createDb factory pool-config shape with pg.Pool + drizzle mocked), comprehensive `README.md` covering naming discipline + forward-only migration policy + per-migration atomicity + online-migration three-step rollout + per-workspace pool isolation + Secret Manager resolution + root db:* / Turbo db:check rationale + Story 1.16c boundary + placeholder sub-directory landing-Story map + packages/db vs packages/domain divergence resolution.

3. **Root pnpm db:* shortcut scripts** authored per Task 3 — `pnpm db:generate / db:migrate / db:check / db:studio` at root `package.json` delegating to `pnpm --filter @twt/domain` (epic AC-1 verbatim wording "pnpm db:migrate applies migrations idempotently"). `turbo.json` `db:check` task wired (inputs: `src/schema/**/*.ts` + `migrations/**/*` + `drizzle.config.ts`; no DB connection required). `db:migrate` is DELIBERATELY NOT a turbo task per architecture §1.8 line 999-1002 "Migration phase precedes code deploy. Schema migrations apply in their own pipeline step; failure stops the pipeline — code is not promoted against an inconsistent schema." The root pnpm script is invoked separately by CI / Dokploy auto-deploy / human operator BEFORE `turbo run build`.

4. **CI `db-check` job** authored per Task 5 — added to `.github/workflows/ci.yml` mirroring the existing `lint` / `typecheck` / `test` / `build` job shape (`needs: install`; runs `pnpm turbo run db:check`; concurrency-grouped). Pure static-analysis pass; no DB connection required. Story 1.2 commits the integrity primitive (drizzle-kit `check`); Story 1.16c commits the substantive v1-baseline forbidden-pattern asserts (no `payout_destinations` table / no `payout_destination*` columns / no `/payout-destinations*` endpoints / no `*PayoutDestination*` Zod schemas per epic line 1338) — the two gates are complementary with disjoint responsibilities. Substantive automated migration-execution-in-CI requires `twt-staging` GCP project + WIF binding + staging Cloud SQL + Dokploy auto-deploy per Story 1.15; deferred.

5. **ADR-0003-datastore-engine drafted** at `docs/adr/ADR-0003-datastore-engine.md` per Task 6.3 — closes the slot `ADR-NNNN-datastore-engine` at `docs/knowledge-transfer/adr-index.md` line 54 (architecture §Deferred Decisions L157-159; expected close trigger = Story 1.2 closure per the indexed expected-close-trigger column). Body covers: (a) engine commitment Postgres 16 + Cloud SQL managed + asia-south1 regional HA + automated backups + PITR transcription from architecture §1.1 + §5.1 + §5.7; (b) ORM choice Drizzle over Prisma + Kysely + MikroORM + Knex with rationale citing architecture line 668 + line 717-718 (Drizzle pgPolicy declarative API support that Story 1.6 RLS depends on) + architecture §1.3 line 776-790 (drizzle-zod type-assignability discipline that Story 1.4 depends on) + Drizzle column-transformer pattern that Story 1.5 envelope encryption depends on + Prisma's lack of native pgPolicy + Prisma's heavier client + Prisma's slower cold-start; (c) drizzle-kit forward-only migration policy transcription per architecture §1.8 + per-Pariwar JSONB per architecture §1.7; (d) Workload Identity Federation + Secret Manager wiring per architecture §5.4 + §5.9. Status: `drafted` at Story 1.2 author-commit; flips to `under-trustee-review` post-Story-1.2-review per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]` lifecycle; ratified per Trustee Panel session (deferred). `adr-index.md` Status row-count table at line 19-26 updated: `slot-reserved-pre-write` 125 → 124, `drafted` 0 → 1; total unchanged at 125.

6. **`docs/escrow/credential-inventory.md` Rows 35-36** status update per Task 6.6 — `cloud-sql-service-account-prod` + `cloud-sql-iam-recovery-grant` envelope availability flipped `pending-system-availability` → `pending-task-7-sealing-event`. The substrate-vs-envelope split is acknowledged: Story 1.2 lands the substrate (Cloud SQL instance + Secret Manager secret + IAM-bound application role + Terraform module that produces them); the substantive envelope-sealing operation requires (a) live `twt-dev-postgres` provisioning execution (deferred to Story 1.2 follow-up per substrate-only execution choice), then (b) BigDev's Story 0.2 Task 7 trustee-execution-time envelope-sealing event. Decision 2026-06-08-038 supersedes the prior `pending-system-availability` framework-leg pose; the live-substantive-availability event remains pending.

7. **Cross-Story discharge triggers fired by Story 1.2 closure:**
   - **Story 1.3** (`packages/events` event-log primitive) — Drizzle client + migration tooling substrate now ready; Story 1.3 authors `events_log` table + Postgres immutability triggers.
   - **Story 1.4** (`packages/contracts` Zod + OpenAPI) — Drizzle schema is the source-of-truth that contract types must stay assignable from per architecture §1.3 line 787-790 + §Naming patterns line 3719-3723.
   - **Story 1.5** (Cloud KMS HSM + Tink envelope encryption per AR-12) — Drizzle column-transformer pattern + `packages/domain/src/encryption/` placeholder ready.
   - **Story 1.6** (`pariwar_id` first-class + RLS adversarial test per AR-3) — Drizzle pgPolicy declarative API + application-role-without-BYPASSRLS posture + `packages/domain/src/policies/` + `src/cross-tenant/` placeholders ready.
   - **Story 1.7** (Pariwar-Passport data model + branding bundle per FR-63) — Drizzle schema + `packages/domain/src/per-pariwar/bihar/` placeholder ready.
   - **Story 1.10** (Tamper-evident audit log hot tier per FR-47 + AR-9/10) — Postgres native partitioning support + trigger-based hash-chain insert path enabled.
   - **Story 1.12** (pg-boss job queue per AR-5) — Postgres instance + `__pgboss` schema isolation pattern per architecture §5.11 line 3424 ready.
   - **Story 1.16c** (schema-diff CI gate per FR-100) — drizzle-kit migration toolchain ready; Story 1.16c builds the v1-baseline forbidden-pattern asserts on top of Story 1.2's `db:check` primitive.

8. **Architecture-vs-Epic-AC `packages/db` workspace-naming divergence resolved** per `[[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]]`. Epic AC line 1016 verbatim wording said "Drizzle schema authoring scaffolding lives in `packages/db`"; architecture §Workspace Layout line 406 + §Complete project directory structure line 4341-4356 + §Naming patterns line 3670-3672 are all canonical: `packages/domain/` is the architecture-committed home ("`packages/domain/` holds the system's identity: Drizzle schema, RLS policies, tenant rules, validators, shared domain types. The database is one expression of the domain, not the system's identity."). Story 1.2 follows architecture (no `packages/db/` workspace created; substrate scaffolds into the pre-existing `packages/domain/` placeholder from Story 1.1). This matches the Story 1.1 "Architecture-vs-Story-body workspace-enumeration divergence" precedent (apps/member non-existence).

9. **Closure-language-precision posture per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`:**
   - **Framework + engineering legs** = **Closed by [edit]** on Tasks 1-7 closure + local CI gates green. Direct objective evidence: Terraform module exists + `pnpm db:migrate` exit 0 with idempotency verified against local Postgres 16 + `pnpm db:check` exit 0 + `pnpm turbo run lint typecheck test build` zero regression vs Story 1.1 baseline + `.github/workflows/ci.yml` db-check job authored.
   - **ADR-0003 trustee-ratification leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** pending Trustee Panel session. ADR-0003 Status flips `drafted` → `under-trustee-review` post-Story-1.2 review; ratified status awaits trustee panel.
   - **Live Cloud SQL provisioning leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** per substrate-only user choice. The substrate is fully authored + tested; live `twt-dev-postgres` provisioning execution + Secret Manager secret-value population + envelope-sealing event are deferred to a follow-up execution as enumerated in `deferred-work.md`.
   - **Story 1.16c forbidden-pattern asserts leg** = **Resolved via explicit deferral** per architecture boundary discipline. Story 1.2 commits the `db:check` primitive; Story 1.16c commits the substantive v1-baseline asserts. The two gates are not competitors — they are complementary commitments under disjoint ownership.

### Decision 2026-06-05-037: ADR-0002 amendment (deploy-key branch-protection exemption) + deferred-work.md cleanup (4 substantive edits + 8 resolved-item annotations) + decision-log template Status-vocab alignment; closes 9 deferred-work items through Phase-0 trustee-ratification propagation

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — deferred-work cleanup + ADR-0002 amendment.

**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed 2026-06-05; cleanup + amendment propagation event from Phase-0 trustee ratification (Decisions 017-036). No new trustee co-sign event required — items closed via citation of prior ratifications.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), discharging deferred-work items that became addressable post-Phase-0-trustee-ratification.

**Ratifying trustees:** Implicit via prior Decisions 2026-06-05-017 + 2026-06-05-018 + 2026-06-05-019 + 2026-06-05-022 + 2026-06-05-023 + 2026-06-05-034 (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti ratified the substantive prerequisites that gated these cleanup items' closures).

**Context.** Post-Phase-0-ratification review of `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` (410 lines) surfaced three categories: (a) items genuinely deferred to future Story/Epic execution (correctly deferred per closure-language-precision discipline); (b) items addressed by the Phase-0 ratification event but still carrying stale `[deferred]` markers; (c) one substantive gap in ADR-0002 surfaced by the review — `git push --mirror` semantics conflict with the ratified branch-protection posture without an explicit deploy-key exemption. This Decision discharges category (b) via 8 annotation events + 4 narrow content edits, and discharges category (c) via an ADR-0002 amendment authored in place.

**Decision body:**

1. **ADR-0002 amendment (substantive gap closure).** Body item 3 amended to specify "GitLab deploy-key whitelist exemption from branch protection" so `git push --mirror` force-push semantics work against the ratified `main` + `release/*` branch-protection posture. Consequences §Operational extended with provisioning step ("GitLab settings: Project → Settings → Repository → Protected branches → Allowed to force push → add deploy-key principal") + first-workflow-run verification step at Task 9. Closes Story 0.3 deferred-work items `§3.5 mirror-side branch-protection blocks --mirror semantics` + `§3.2 mirror destination ref-deletion blocked by mirror-side policy`. `mirror-procedure.md §1 Prerequisites` updated in parallel to cite the amendment. No supersession event on ADR-0002 — amendment closes a substantive gap in the original ratification without changing the chosen control set (destination + credential model + branch protection + rotation cadence all unchanged). ADR-0002 Changelog row appended noting the 2026-06-05b amendment.

2. **Four narrow content edits applied (post-ratification cleanup):**

   - **`docs/escrow/credential-inventory.md` Domain 5 trust-bank row** — Availability-status hedge "confirm with Trustee Panel during Task 7 execution that trust bank credentials are operationally held and ready for escrow" lifted; replaced with definitive citation `sealable-now per .decision-log.md Decision 2026-06-05-018 + ADR-0001 (mechanism + custodial paths ratified)`. Closes Story 0.2 deferred-work item `Trust-bank operational pre-existence cite is light`.
   - **`.decision-log.md` Template for new entries — Status field vocabulary** — original template offered `Ratified | Author-committed; awaiting trustee sign-off | Reversed by Decision YYYY-MM-DD-NNN`; updated to `Trustee-ratified | Author-committed; awaiting trustee sign-off | Solo-builder-author-committed | Reversed by Decision YYYY-MM-DD-NNN | Superseded by Decision YYYY-MM-DD-NNN`. Reconciles the inconsistency between template's `Ratified` and operational usage `Trustee-ratified` across Decisions 017-036. Closes Story 0.2 deferred-work item `Status vocab drift (Ratified vs Trustee-ratified)`.
   - **`docs/escrow/escrow-ledger.md` Authority section** — Mirror-coverage stance updated from "pending" to "ratified; substantive execution in flight" with substantive citations to Decision 2026-06-05-019 + ADR-0002 + Decision 2026-06-05-037 (branch-protection exemption amendment). Closes Story 0.3 deferred-work item `Story 0.2's escrow-ledger.md Mirror coverage section not updated by Story 0.3`.
   - **`docs/degradation-policy/table-top-exercise.md` Day-7+1 trustee-action narrative** — original "if Story 0.6 has closed... if Story 0.2 has progressed past Task 6..." conditional hedges replaced with definitive citations of Decisions 022 + 018 + ADR-0001 + gap-language matching the current state (frameworks ratified; substantive execution per Tasks 7+/10+). Closes Story 0.4 deferred-work item `Emergency-override chain (Day 5) via Story 0.2 escrow may be inoperable if escrow not yet sealed`.

3. **Eight deferred-work items annotated as RESOLVED per Phase-0 trustee ratification:**

   | Item | Originating story | Resolved by Decision |
   |---|---|---|
   | High-sensitivity §5.9 / sealing trustees alignment | Story 0.2 | 2026-06-05-018 (Q2.6) + ADR-0001 body item 5 |
   | Redundant-custody sync drift | Story 0.2 | 2026-06-05-018 (Q2.1+Q2.2+Q2.3) + ADR-0001 single-location-per-class |
   | `docs/adr/` directory does not exist | Story 0.2 | 2026-06-05-034 (QC.3) + 2026-06-05-005 (Story 0.5 Task 1 scaffolding) + 2026-06-05-019 (Q3.3) |
   | §3.1 auth-failure recovery requires Story 0.2 re-seal | Story 0.3 | 2026-06-05-018 + ADR-0001 (Story 0.2 Task 6 closed) |
   | §3.5 mirror-side branch-protection blocks --mirror | Story 0.3 | 2026-06-05-037 (this Decision) + ADR-0002 amendment |
   | §3.2 mirror destination ref-deletion blocked | Story 0.3 | 2026-06-05-037 (this Decision) + ADR-0002 amendment |
   | Two pending-ADR rows + workflow only supports one | Story 0.3 | 2026-06-05-019 (Q3.1) + ADR-0002 single-destination |
   | Pre-Task-8 workflow failures pile up with no auto-log | Story 0.3 (PARTIAL) | 2026-06-05-019 (Q3.7) — routing confirmed to Story 1.16x; substantive wiring deferred |
   | {fallback_handler_phone} P0-1-pending placeholder | Story 0.4 | 2026-06-05-023 (Q7.1+Q7.3+QA.2) — Trustee-on-rota substantive backfill for 4 BENCH loop nodes |
   | Emergency-override chain (Day 5) Story 0.2 escrow inoperable | Story 0.4 (PARTIAL) | 2026-06-05-018 + ADR-0001 (Task 6 closed; Task 7 sealing pending) |

4. **Items NOT resolved at this Decision** (correctly remain deferred per closure-language-precision):

   - All Story 1.16a/1.16b/1.16x CI gate items (Story 0.2 atomic three-way commit; ledger chain/sig integrity; cross-reference link checking; sole-Solo-Builder admin enforcement) — deferred to Epic 1 Story 1.16x execution.
   - All operations-policy items (time-bounded provisional closure for AC-3; periodic re-verification cadence; ledger-vs-workflow reconciliation interval; operations policy authoring for re-sign threshold granularity; etc.) — deferred to operations-policy authoring event.
   - All Story 0.6 Task 10 / Story 0.13 Task 11 / Story 1.1 / 1.5 / 1.10 dependencies — deferred to substantive execution per Decision 2026-06-05-035 body item 4 cross-Story full-closure conditions.
   - Story 0.7 W-* items (rota schema 2-slot limit; bench-on-leave; Operations Lead temporary absence; peer-mesh density; reconciliation day-N; UPI surge) — deferred to operational evidence + ADR slots reserved in Story 0.7 README §8.
   - Stories 0.8/0.9/0.10/0.11 Tasks 7-11 execution + 30+ deferred ADR slots for research methodology — deferred to substantive empathy field-work execution.
   - Story 0.12 Task 11 Step 4 implementation-readiness validation; Month-3 re-attestation — deferred to Solo Builder execution + Month-3 trigger.
   - Story 0.13 Tasks 8-11 counsel selection + signature + first artifact + returns — deferred to Story 0.13 execution per Decision 029.
   - Story 0.14 Tasks 8-11 prototype + measurement + ratify-or-pivot — deferred to Solo Builder execution per Decision 030.
   - Story 0.15 Tasks 9-11 monthly review + per-row closure + final sign-off + annual re-attestation — deferred to Story 0.15 cadence per Decisions 031 + 033.
   - Story 0.15 W-02 ADR pre-staging narrative for 6 unauthored Section L slots (slippage-tolerance, trust formation decomposition, conditional-escalation predicate materialization, monthly-review meeting cadence, emergency-review trigger, ≥2-trustee quorum fallback) — partially addressed (ADR-0001 + ADR-0002 narrative landed; 6 other slots remain `slot-reserved-pre-write`); deferred to authoring events.

5. **No new ADR authored at this Decision.** ADR-0002 amendment is in-place per the ADR README §Lifecycle "ratified ADR can carry minor amendments without supersession if the chosen control set is unchanged"; this Decision records the amendment cross-reference.

6. **No sprint-status flip required.** All Phase-0 stories at `done` per Decision 036; epic-0 rollup status unchanged per user-pending decision.

### Decision 2026-06-05-036: Sprint-status flip — Stories 0.1 + 0.2 + 0.3 in-progress → done; closure-language-precision pattern alignment with Stories 0.4-0.15; substantive operational execution remains deferred per deferred-work.md + inventory-roster.md

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — sprint-status closure-state alignment.

**Status:** Solo-builder-author-committed 2026-06-05; ratified-by-prior-Decisions per closure-language-precision pattern propagation.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), discharging an oversight in Decisions 2026-06-05-017 + 2026-06-05-018 + 2026-06-05-019 which ratified the Tasks 4-11 paths for Stories 0.1/0.2/0.3 but did NOT include explicit `sprint-status.yaml` flip directives.

**Ratifying trustees:** Implicit via Decisions 2026-06-05-017 + 2026-06-05-018 + 2026-06-05-019 (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti ratified the substantive trustee-input prerequisite that gated the framework-Closed → `done` transition). No additional ≥2-trustee co-sign event required for this sprint-status alignment per closure-language-precision precedent established for Stories 0.4-0.15.

**Context.** Stories 0.1 + 0.2 + 0.3 were the **earliest Phase-0 stories** (frameworks author-committed via Decisions 2026-05-29-001 + 2026-05-29-002 + 2026-05-29-003). At that time the closure-language-precision discipline — "framework Closed by [edit] + trustee-ratified = sprint-status `done`; substantive operational execution deferred per closure-language" — was not yet firmly established as a sprint-status flip convention. Stories 0.4 onward (Decisions 004/005/006/007/008/009/010/011/013/014/015) adopted this convention from the start and flipped to `done` at framework + trustee ratification despite having identical `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION_` Tasks 7+ structures (trustee sign-offs, dry-runs, table-tops, comprehension administrations, counsel returns, native-stack experiments).

The trustee ratification event of 2026-06-04 → 2026-06-05 (Decisions 017-035) discharged the trustee-input prerequisite for Stories 0.1/0.2/0.3, but Decisions 017/018/019 themselves did not include explicit `sprint-status.yaml` flip directives (unlike Decision 028 body item 10 which explicitly directed `sprint-status.yaml:99 flipped review → done` for Story 0.12). This Decision discharges the oversight by aligning the three earliest Phase-0 stories' `sprint-status` state with the convention applied to 0.4-0.15.

**Decision body:**

1. **`sprint-status.yaml` per-story flips applied (lines 88-90):**
   - `0-1-operational-runbooks-authored-trustee-signed: in-progress` → `done` per framework Closed by [edit] (Decision 2026-05-29-001) + trustee ratification (Decision 2026-06-05-017).
   - `0-2-credential-escrow-established-with-trustee-quorum-open: in-progress` → `done` per framework Closed by [edit] (Decision 2026-05-29-002) + trustee ratification (Decision 2026-06-05-018) + ADR-0001 substantive authoring (Decision 2026-06-05-034).
   - `0-3-code-escrow-auto-mirror-pipeline-live: in-progress` → `done` per framework Closed by [edit] (Decision 2026-05-29-003) + trustee ratification (Decision 2026-06-05-019) + ADR-0002 substantive authoring (Decision 2026-06-05-034).

2. **Closure-language precision applied** per [[feedback_closure_language_precision]]: the sprint-status `done` reflects **framework-Closed + trustee-ratified** state, NOT substantive operational execution discharge. The substantive operational execution items for each Story remain tracked per the deferred-work + launch-gate-inventory surfaces:
   - **Story 0.1 substantive execution deferred:** 14 trustee sign-off events (≥2 trustees × 7 runbooks) per Task 4 + substitute-engineer walk-through of each runbook in non-load-bearing target per Task 5 (substitute-engineer identity to be nominated by Trustee Panel per Q1.3). Tracked per `docs/runbooks/operational-readiness-ledger.md` Sign-off log + Execution-validation log + cross-Story dependency on Story 0.6 backup engineer per Q1.3 + Decision 022 for full closure of AC-4 path 1.
   - **Story 0.2 substantive execution deferred:** Tasks 7-9 (initial sealing of `sealable-now` envelopes per ADR-0001 + dry-run quorum-open per envelope class per Q2.4 amendment + bus-factor table-top per Q2.8). Tracked per `docs/escrow/escrow-ledger.md` Sealing log + Dry-run log + Bus-factor table-top log. AC-2 full closure requires Stories 1.10 (audit-mirror substrate) + 1.5 (KEK substrate) per the per-envelope-class dry-run discipline — those are Epic 1 substrate-development tracked in their respective Story records.
   - **Story 0.3 substantive execution deferred:** Tasks 8-11 (GitLab.com trustee-owned foundation account provisioning + SSH deploy key generation + `MIRROR_PUSH_CREDENTIAL` + `MIRROR_DESTINATION_URL` + `KNOWN_HOSTS` secret wiring + first release-branch push + ≥2-trustee read-access verification + restoration drill + bus-factor switch-to-mirror exercise). Tracked per `docs/escrow/code-escrow/code-escrow-ledger.md` Mirror destination ratification log + Workflow-secret-wiring log + Mirror-workflow run record + Read-access verification log + Restoration drill log + Bus-factor switch-to-mirror log. AC-2 full closure requires Story 0.6 backup engineer per Q3.8 + Q3.9 + Decision 022 for the bus-factor executor role; restoration-drill meaningful exercise requires Story 1.1 Turborepo bootstrap to land for `pnpm install` + apps/* build to be substantive.

3. **No retroactive supersession on Decisions 017/018/019.** Those Decisions ratified the substantive trustee-input prerequisites and remain operationally binding. This Decision is a downstream propagation event aligning sprint-status state, not a Decision-supersession event on the trustee ratifications themselves.

4. **Cross-Story coupling preserved.** Per Decision 2026-06-05-035 body item 4 cross-Story full-closure conditions enumeration, the substantive-execution items for Stories 0.1/0.2/0.3 continue to be tracked as provisional-closure-pending-substantive-execution per `deferred-work.md`. The sprint-status `done` does NOT mean those items are complete; it means the framework + trustee-ratification gates have fired and the items are tracked as deferred-execution per the closure-language-precision discipline.

5. **`sprint-status.yaml` header `last_updated` field updated** to `2026-06-05b` with rationale text referencing this Decision; prior `2026-06-05` ratification-event header preserved in the `|`-separated history per the file's append-only header convention.

6. **No additional cross-Story ledger updates required** at this Decision. The Story 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.3 ledgers (`docs/runbooks/operational-readiness-ledger.md` + `docs/escrow/escrow-ledger.md` + `docs/escrow/code-escrow/code-escrow-ledger.md`) already record the framework-Closed state per their respective Decisions 001/002/003 + Tasks 7+ AWAITING markers per their respective ratification Decisions 017/018/019. The `inventory-roster.md` Row tracking per Story 0.15 is unaffected — Story 0.15 monthly review cadence per Decision 031 + first review 2026-07-01 will surface the Stories 0.1/0.2/0.3 substantive-execution progress as the relevant Tasks land.

**Open follow-ups:**

- None at the sprint-status level. Substantive operational execution items continue per the per-Story ledgers + the Phase-0 critical-path priority per Decision 035 body item 7.
- Trustee Panel monthly review per Decision 031 first meeting 2026-07-01 will audit substantive-execution progress across all Phase-0 Stories including 0.1/0.2/0.3.
- Convention-establishment commitment: future Story-N trustee-ratification Decisions should explicitly include `sprint-status.yaml` flip directives in body items (per Decision 028 body item 10 precedent) to avoid this kind of alignment-gap from recurring.

### Decision 2026-06-05-035: Phase-0 provisional closure ratified per QX.2 — substitute paths accepted across Phase-0 portfolio; Phase-0 prereq-gate fires; Epic 1 substrate work unblocked; full closure deferred per `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` cross-Story coupling

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — Phase-0 closure meta-event.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05 by ≥2-trustee panel (Dhiraj Rahul + Kalpana Bharti) via `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` sign-off (74 trustee decisions across original + 1st-addendum + 2nd-addendum closed).

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QX.1 + QX.2 + QX.3 + Phase-0 closure context.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2). Both signed all three sign-off blocks of `phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` dated 2026-06-04 / 2026-06-05.

**Context.** The Phase-0 trustee questionnaire (32 original + 28 first-addendum + 14 second-addendum = 74 decisions across 13 stories) was authored 2026-06-04 in response to the Story 0.12 Task 9 trustee-acknowledgement requirement (Decision 2026-06-04-016 supersedes to ratified at Decision 2026-06-05-028 below). The questionnaire surfaced trustee-decision items across all Phase-0 stories: in-progress (0.1, 0.2, 0.3), review (0.12), and `done`-per-framework but with `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION_` Tasks 7+ (0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.10, 0.11, 0.13, 0.14, 0.15). Per QX.2 = "Provisionally closed", Phase-0 stories accept substitute paths where Story 0.6 backup-engineer execution / Story 1.10 audit-mirror substrate / Story 1.5 KEK-tier substrate are pre-conditions for full closure. Per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`, each Story's ledger row records "Provisionally closed by [edit/sign-off]; full closure deferred per [...]" rather than collapsing the two states.

**Decision body:**

1. **QX.1 — Story 0.6 backup engineer urgency.** Trustees prioritize the Story 0.6 backup engineer's first table-top execution for the next phase post Phase-0 sign-off. Story 0.6 framework-leg fully closes via Decision 2026-06-05-022 below; named-engineer outreach begins per Q6.3 hybrid path; AC-2 activation-scenario test (Story 0.1 AC-4 path 1 discharge) follows once Story 0.6 Task 10 lands the contracted engineer.

2. **QX.2 — Phase-0 closure language.** Phase-0 closes as **Provisionally closed** per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`. Substitute paths accepted; full closure deferred per `deferred-work.md`. Phase-0 prereq-gate per `epics.md` line 564 ("Phase-0 prereq gates gate all engineering") fires; Epic 1 substrate work (Story 1.1 Turborepo bootstrap onwards) is unblocked subject to per-Story provisional-closure dispositions enumerated in Decisions 2026-06-05-017 through 2026-06-05-034.

3. **QX.3 — Trustee ratification mechanics.** Inline trustee initials in `phase-0-trustee-questionnaire.md` + document committed to repo + this `.decision-log.md` transcription event (Decisions 017-035) constitute the canonical ratification record. Per-Story Decision supersessions reference back to the questionnaire as Source-of-Truth for trustee answers.

4. **Cross-Story full-closure conditions** enumerated in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md`. Stories needing post-Phase-0 substantive execution for full closure: 0.1 (AC-4 substitute engineer + full via 0.6); 0.2 (envelope-class dry-runs full via 1.10 + 1.5); 0.3 (AC-2 bus-factor switch-to-mirror full via 0.6); 0.4 (AC-1 comms-template ratification full via 0.13; AC-2 table-top full via 0.6); 0.5 (AC-3 comprehension administration full via 0.6 Task 10); 0.6 (AC-1 contract + IAM + onboarding via Task 9-11; AC-2 activation via Task 12); 0.7 (per-loop-node ratification per QA.2 disposition + 23-row backfill via 90-day substitute-bench window); 0.8/0.9/0.10/0.11 (recruitment + conduct + synthesis + reviewer-of-record sign-offs via Tasks 7-11); 0.13 (counsel selection + engagement-letter signature via Tasks 7-11); 0.14 (prototype build + measurement + ratify-or-pivot via Tasks 8-11); 0.15 (monthly review cadence + per-row closure events + Phase 1 launch readiness signal via Tasks 9-11).

5. **30-day takeover joint-discharge entry** per Decision 2026-06-05-032 (QC.1) fires when Stories 0.3 + 0.4 + 0.5 + 0.6 conditions all materialize. Solo Builder records the entry; ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via reviewer-of-record convention per QC.1 ratification.

6. **Phase-0 provisional closure does NOT retroactively change** any Phase-0 Story's `sprint-status.yaml` status. Status entries reflect framework-leg closure per author-commit. Substantive completion is tracked in `docs/launch-gate-inventory/inventory-roster.md` per Decision 2026-06-05-031 (Story 0.15 ratification).

7. **Critical-path priority after sign-off** per questionnaire lines 1009-1014: (1) Story 0.6 A-13 retainer authorization unblock execution (Decision 022); (2) Story 0.7 substitute-handler-bench start 2026-06-05 (Decision 023); (3) Story 0.13 counsel outreach kickoff (Decision 029); (4) Story 0.14 native-stack experiment kickoff (Decision 030); (5) Story 0.15 first monthly review 2026-07-01 (Decision 031); (6) Stories 0.8/0.9/0.10/0.11 empathy field-work execution per pre-recruitment approvals (Decisions 024-027).

### Decision 2026-06-05-034: QC.3 ratified — sealing-mechanism ADR (Story 0.2) + code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR (Story 0.3) authored now; counsel-return-not-required; closes Story 0.2 Task 6 + Story 0.3 Task 7 ADR-legs fully

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — ADR-authoring timing.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.3.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.3 asked whether the credential-escrow-mechanism ADR (Story 0.2 Task 6) and the code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR (Story 0.3 Task 7) should be authored NOW or wait for Story 0.13 counsel return. Trustees confirmed (a) Author ADR now — the custodial-mechanism + mirror-destination decisions are settled per Decisions 2026-06-05-018 (sealing) + 2026-06-05-019 (mirror); they do NOT require legal counsel input.

**Decision body:**

1. **Credential-escrow-mechanism ADR — author now.** Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism.md` capturing: sealing mechanism = physical sealed envelopes in joint bank safe deposit (per Decision 018 / Q2.1); custodial location = trustee residences + bank safe deposit boxes (Q2.2); §2.10a control = separate custodial path per envelope class (Q2.3) — `prod-credential` envelopes at trustee residences; `audit-mirror-credential` envelopes at bank safe deposit; `high-sensitivity-tier-credential` envelopes at structurally distinct path per ADR specification; rotation/re-seal interface; dry-run repeatability; geographic-resilience property; §2.10a separability rationale (trustee-headcount = 3 per Q2.7 forces non-disjoint-subset mechanism; separate custodial path is the operative control).

2. **Code-escrow-mirror-destination ADR — author now.** Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination.md` capturing: destination = GitLab.com under trustee-owned foundation account (per Decision 019 / Q3.1); credential model = SSH deploy key with push-only scope, stored as GitHub Actions secret `MIRROR_PUSH_CREDENTIAL` (Q3.2); branch protection enforced (no force-push; signed commits; ≥1 trustee approval per Q3.5); rotation cadence = annual (Q3.6); host-key pinning mechanism per Story 0.3 OQ 9 SSH TOFU resolution; geographic-resilience property (GitLab.com SaaS, cross-cloud independent of `asia-south1`).

3. **`docs/adr/` directory scaffolding.** Per Q3.3 ratified Story 0.3 scaffolds `docs/adr/` (template + index) as part of Task 7 ADR authoring. The scaffold lands concurrently with ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism + ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination as the first two substantive ADRs. Story 0.15 architectural launch-gate inventory inventory-roster Row for ADR scaffolding flips from `open` to `closed`.

4. **Closure-language precision** per `[[feedback_closure_language_precision]]`: Story 0.2 Task 6 + Story 0.3 Task 7 ADR-legs flip from `Resolved via explicit deferral pending ADR authoring` to `Closed by [edit]`. The substantive ADR landings replace the `[deferred ADR — placeholder procedure]` tags in `docs/escrow/sealing-procedure.md` §2 + `docs/escrow/code-escrow/mirror-procedure.md` §2 + `.github/workflows/code-escrow-mirror.yml` Step 2 inline comment.

5. **Counsel-input scope clarification.** This Decision establishes that custodial-mechanism + mirror-destination ADRs are NOT legal instruments requiring counsel return. Counsel return per Story 0.13 remains required for: comms-template ratification (Story 0.4 Task 9 × 5 templates); backup-engineer contract substantive language (Story 0.6 Task 9 §6 NDA + §9 Insurance + §10 Termination + §11 Dispute resolution); ADR-NNNN-threat-model-actor-inventory + ADR-NNNN-Cloudflare-pivot + ADR-NNNN-Hindi-native-trustee-ratification + ADR-NNNN-backup-engineer-contract-substantive-language + ADR-NNNN-engineer-identity-redaction-public-mirror (Story 0.5 ADR slot population × 5 — counsel-co-signed); DPO-breach-reporting envelope substantive content (Story 0.2 deferred row).

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder authors `docs/adr/` scaffolding + ADR-NNNN-credential-escrow-mechanism + ADR-NNNN-code-escrow-mirror-destination within the Decision 035 commit (or as a follow-up commit cited from this Decision).
- Story 0.2 sealing-procedure + Story 0.3 mirror-procedure + workflow YAML inline comment updates land in the same commit, removing `[deferred ADR — placeholder procedure]` tags.
- Story 0.15 inventory-roster row for ADR scaffolding flips to `closed` per Decision 031.

### Decision 2026-06-05-033: QC.2 ratified — annual re-attestation cadence start date 2027-07-01 for Story 0.5 KT pack + Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory; 12-month interval from Q15.3 first monthly review

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — Annual re-attestation cadence.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.2.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.2 set the annual re-attestation cadence start date for surfaces that require annual re-attestation: Story 0.5 KT pack (`docs/knowledge-transfer/kt-pack-ledger.md` "Annual re-attestation log") + Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory (`docs/launch-gate-inventory/engagement-ledger.md` §7 annual re-attestation log). Both surfaces commit annual re-attestation per their README §6 / README §6 review cadence fallbacks.

**Decision body:**

1. **Default annual re-attestation date = 2027-07-01.** Anchored to Q15.3 first monthly review date (2026-07-01) + 12 months. The 12-month interval applies symmetrically to both surfaces.

2. **Re-attestation scope per surface.** Story 0.5 KT pack: Trustee Panel walks the seven KT pack components + the comprehension questionnaire + the third-party-dependency-inventory + the ADR-slot status; confirms component freshness; identifies revision needs; records per-component re-attestation rows in `kt-pack-ledger.md`. Story 0.15 launch-gate inventory: Trustee Panel walks all 15 inventory-roster rows; confirms closed-row evidence still holds; accepts-risk rows still valid; deferred-per-named-criteria predicates still operative; reframed dispositions still consistent; records per-row annual re-attestation entries in `engagement-ledger.md §7`.

3. **Re-attestation triggers OUTSIDE annual cadence** remain operative per each Story's README §6: major-architecture-amendment triggers re-baseline; per-Epic estimation drift threshold triggers mid-cycle re-attestation; major-incident triggers ad-hoc re-attestation; explicit Trustee Panel discretion can convene a re-attestation at any time.

4. **First-cycle re-attestation 2027-07-01 anchors all subsequent cycles** at 2028-07-01, 2029-07-01, … unless superseded by a re-attestation-cadence amendment recorded as a new `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry referencing this Decision.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder appends placeholder rows in `kt-pack-ledger.md` "Annual re-attestation log" + `engagement-ledger.md §7` for the 2027-07-01 first cycle.
- Trustee Panel schedules the 2027-07-01 session at a reasonable lead time (recommended: ≥30 days advance notice per `monthly-review-cadence-protocol.md §4` precedent).
- Monthly review cadence per Decision 2026-06-05-031 (Story 0.15 Q15.3) continues monthly through the year; the annual re-attestation is additive, not a replacement.

### Decision 2026-06-05-032: QC.1 ratified — 30-day takeover joint-discharge entry recorded by Solo Builder when Stories 0.3+0.4+0.5+0.6 eight-condition union materializes; ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via reviewer-of-record convention

**Decision type:** Cross-Story Phase-0 governance — 30-day takeover joint-discharge convention.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of QC.1.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** QC.1 codified the convention for recording the 30-day takeover joint-discharge entry. Stories 0.3 + 0.4 + 0.5 + 0.6 each commit a follow-up `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry recording the achievement when the eight-condition union closes:

> Story 0.3 AC-1 + AC-2 ∧ Story 0.4 AC-1 + AC-2 ∧ Story 0.5 AC-1 + AC-2 + AC-3 ∧ Story 0.6 AC-1 + AC-2

**Decision body:**

1. **Recording authority.** Solo Builder records the joint-discharge `.decision-log.md` `[CONTINUITY]` entry when all eight conditions materialize. The entry is authored as a single supersession referencing Decisions 2026-05-29-003 (Story 0.3) + 2026-05-29-004 (Story 0.4) + 2026-05-30-005 (Story 0.5) + 2026-05-30-006 (Story 0.6) + 2026-06-05-019 (Story 0.3 ratification) + 2026-06-05-020 (Story 0.4 ratification) + 2026-06-05-021 (Story 0.5 ratification) + 2026-06-05-022 (Story 0.6 ratification).

2. **Acknowledgment threshold = ≥1 trustee via reviewer-of-record convention.** Distinct from the ≥2-trustee co-sign discipline applied to individual Decision supersessions. Rationale: the joint-discharge entry is a *recording-of-achieved-state* event (each constituent Decision already carries its own trustee ratification), not a *new-decision* event. ≥1-trustee acknowledgment per the empathy-portfolio-reviewer-of-record convention (Decisions 008-011) is the operative threshold.

3. **Trustee-of-record nomination at recording time.** Solo Builder requests ≥1-trustee acknowledgment via the Trustee Panel chair on rota at the recording event. The acknowledging trustee records the date + ack signature in the entry. If no trustee is available, the entry is held in draft state until ≥1-trustee acknowledgment lands.

4. **Cross-link discipline.** The joint-discharge entry cross-links to: `docs/runbooks/operational-readiness-ledger.md` "30-day takeover joint-discharge" section; `docs/escrow/code-escrow/code-escrow-ledger.md` "Restoration drill + switch-to-mirror" entries; `docs/degradation-policy/degradation-policy-ledger.md` "Table-top exercise" entries; `docs/knowledge-transfer/kt-pack-ledger.md` "Comprehension administration" entries; `docs/backup-engineer/backup-engineer-ledger.md` "Activation event" entry.

5. **Expected timing per QX.2 = provisional Phase-0 closure.** Eight-condition union likely materializes in Q2/Q3 of Epic 1 timeframe (post-Story-0.6 backup-engineer table-top execution + post-Stories-1.10 / 1.5 substrate-dependent dry-runs). Solo Builder tracks progress in `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/deferred-work.md` cross-Story coupling section.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder monitors eight-condition union progress as Phase-0 stories advance from provisional to full closure.
- When the union materializes, Solo Builder authors the joint-discharge entry per the schema above and requests ≥1-trustee acknowledgment.

### Decision 2026-06-05-029: Story 0.13 Tasks 7-11 ratified — engagement-scope pack-as-a-unit + counsel-roster shortlist criteria confirmed + outreach paths (Trustee Panel network + Bar Council of Bihar + existing legal-counsel contacts) + ≥2 candidates required + standalone funding (Story 0.12 no-trigger) + engagement budget envelope ₹15k retainer / ₹7.5k standard / ₹15k expedited / ₹300k annual cap; supersession of Decision 2026-06-02-013 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.13 — Legal counsel engagement.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q13.1-Q13.7.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul, Kalpana Bharti.

**Context.** Story 0.13 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-06-02-013. Tasks 7-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified Task 7 scope + Task 8 shortlist criteria + Task 9 budget disposition via Q13.1-Q13.7.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q13.1 — Engagement-scope ratification mode = pack-as-a-unit.** Scope items ratified as a single unit: (a) review-scope-charter §1 five AC items; (b) ~32-row cross-Story deferred-scope inventory; (c) regulatory-surface review; (d) ADR slot review; (e) pre-launch checkpoint coverage; (f) out-of-scope items; (g) counsel-roster shortlist criteria. Single ledger entry; this Decision is the supersession.

2. **Q13.2 — Counsel-roster shortlist criteria confirmed as drafted.** Mandatory: DPDPA practice; Indian Trust Act practice; concurrent-review-mode availability; no prior TSCT engagement; professional-indemnity insurance coverage. Preferred: financial-services regulatory; Bihar / Hindi context familiarity; mutual-aid trust track record.

3. **Q13.3 — Outreach paths = Trustee Panel professional network + Bar Council of Bihar referrals + existing legal-counsel contacts of board members.** Published-practice DPDPA experts in Hindi-language jurisdictions NOT in scope at this stage (may be added per subsequent supersession if first-pass shortlist yields fewer than ≥2 qualified candidates).

4. **Q13.4 — Single-candidate selection NOT permitted.** Strict ≥2 candidates per shortlist required; if first-pass yields fewer than ≥2 candidates meeting mandatory criteria, outreach paths expand per supersession entry (e.g., add Q13.3 option (d) published-practice DPDPA experts).

5. **Q13.5 — Funding cross-coupling resolved = separate dedicated legal-counsel funding allocation.** Story 0.12 reconciliation closed at no-trigger (no contract-help routing) per Decision 2026-06-04-016. Story 0.13 budget per Q13.6 is funded via a dedicated trust operating-budget line item (not standalone-fund per Story 0.12 contract-help-path-N/A). Decision 2026-06-02-013 body item 9 funding cross-coupling resolved.

6. **Q13.6 — Engagement budget envelope.** Retainer = ₹15,000/month (availability compensation; on-call regular access to counsel for clarifications + short-answer routine queries). Per-artifact standard pricing = ₹7,500/artifact (5-10 biz days return per `engagement-letter-template.md §4`). Per-artifact expedited pricing = ₹15,000/artifact (2-3 biz days surge). Annual cap = ₹300,000 (covers retainer ₹180k + up to ~16 standard artifacts ₹120k, OR fewer expedited artifacts; cap-breach triggers supersession + Trustee Panel re-ratification).

7. **Q13.7 — Emergency single-trustee scope-ratification fallback confirmed as drafted.** README §5 fallback valid under documented trustee incapacitation, time-bounded 30 days per Story 0.9 D-02 + Story 0.7 README §5 precedent; recorded as `.decision-log.md` `[LEGAL]` entry per supersession schema.

8. **Cross-Story unblock multiplier.** Story 0.13 Task 11 counsel returns unblock: Story 0.4 Task 9 comms-template ratification (×5 channels: push + WhatsApp + SMS + email + public-page-banner); Story 0.6 Task 9 contract template substantive language (§6 NDA + §9 Insurance + §10 Termination + §11 Dispute resolution); Story 0.5 ADR slot population × 5 (threat-model-actor-inventory + Cloudflare-pivot + Hindi-native-trustee-ratification + backup-engineer-contract-substantive-language + engineer-identity-redaction-public-mirror); Story 0.2 DPO-breach-reporting envelope substantive content. Cross-references per Decision 028 (Story 0.12 Task 9 ratification body item 7) funding-tradeoff resolution.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Solo Builder + Trustee Panel execute counsel shortlist outreach per Q13.3 paths within 30 days of this Decision (target outreach kickoff date 2026-06-15).
- Trustee Panel + Solo Builder conduct candidate interviews per Q13.4 ≥2-candidate discipline; selection per `engagement-ledger.md §4` Counsel-selection log.

### Decision 2026-06-05-027: Story 0.11 Tasks 7-11 ratified — host helpline institution = TSCT + pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval (≥2-trustee both initialed) + Both trustees joint TERMINAL reviewer-of-record for P0-2 portfolio sign-off; supersession of Decision 2026-05-31-011 framework-leg

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q11.1-Q11.3.

Story 0.11 contributes the FOURTH and TERMINAL of four P0-2 legs.

1. **Q11.1 — Host helpline institution choice = TSCT.** TSCT selected per familiar context + established trustee relationship. NSCT (sibling-trust) + analogous-trust paths NOT in scope at this stage; may be added per subsequent supersession if TSCT consent declined.

Approval covers: ≥4-hour shadowing across ≥2 distinct shifts; observer-not-participant discipline; per-call caller spoken-and-recorded consent; caller-revocation-mid-call discipline; emotional-overload-observer-discretion-end discipline. Both trustees initial (Trustee 1 = `dr`, Trustee 2 = `kp`) per Second Addendum sign-off block.

3. **Q11.3 — Reviewer-of-record = BOTH trustees joint TERMINAL sign-off.** Recommended given P0-2 portfolio-discharge weight. Both trustees co-review synthesis at `_bmad-output/research/p0-2d-operator-shadowing.md`; both sign off; Decision 2026-05-31-011 supersession authored as joint sign-off entry.

4. **P0-2 portfolio TERMINAL leg discharge trigger.** Task 11 closure (post Task 10 joint sign-off) discharges the **P0-2 four-leg portfolio (0.8 + 0.9 + 0.10 + 0.11) as TERMINAL** — final empathy-portfolio sign-off in Phase-0. A supersession entry on this Decision records the portfolio-TERMINAL discharge event with cross-references to Decisions 2026-06-05-024 (0.8) + 2026-06-05-025 (0.9) + 2026-06-05-026 (0.10).

5. **Task 7 execution sequencing.** Solo Builder approaches TSCT leadership for host-institution consent (PRECONDITION-1). If consent landed, Solo Builder recruits 1 operator via host-institution-mediated path (operator written consent obtained BEFORE shadowing begins). Per-call caller spoken-and-recorded consent obtained at the start of each observed call. Tasks 8-9 execution per `_bmad-output/research/p0-2d-operator-shadowing-protocol/`.

### Decision 2026-06-05-025: Story 0.9 Tasks 7-11 ratified — pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval WITH CONSTRAINT (90-day-post-bereavement + voluntary + stop-anytime safeguard; ≥2-trustee both initialed) + Dhiraj facilitates trustee-mediated path via TSCT network + Both trustees panel-wide reviewer-of-record (Pattern 4 grammar evaluation); supersession of Decision 2026-05-31-009 framework-leg

**Decision type:** Story 0.9 — P0-2b Bereaved-spouse conversation.

**Status:** Trustee-ratified 2026-06-05.

**Author:** Solo Builder (BigDev), transcription of Q9.1-Q9.3.

**Ratifying trustees:** Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1, initial `dr` on Q9.1), Kalpana Bharti (Trustee 2, initial `kp` on Q9.1).

**Context.** Story 0.9 framework-leg author-committed via Decision 2026-05-31-009. Tasks 7-11 parked at `_AWAITING EXTERNAL ACTION`. Trustees ratified Task 7 PRECONDITION (pre-conversation panel approval) + trustee-mediated recruitment path + Task 10 reviewer-of-record via Q9.1-Q9.3. Bereavement-context sensitivity escalates above 0.8's recruit-first model.

**Decision body:**

1. **Q9.1 — Pre-recruitment Trustee Panel approval = APPROVED WITH CONSTRAINT (both trustees initial).** Approve the bereaved-spouse conversation in principle subject to the following ethics safeguard: **Require trustee confirmation that the bereavement event occurred at least 90 days prior to recruitment and that participation is entirely voluntary with an explicit right to stop the conversation at any time.** Approval otherwise covers: ≥60-min Hindi conversation at spouse's chosen setting; re-consent-for-quotation discipline per ethics-protocol §2; Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation. Both trustees initial (Trustee 1 = `dr`, Trustee 2 = `kp`) per Second Addendum sign-off block.

2. **Q9.2 — Trustee-mediated recruitment path = Dhiraj Rahul facilitates via TSCT network.** Solo Builder does NOT approach bereaved spouses directly; Dhiraj Rahul (Trustee 1) facilitates the introduction via TSCT network. Q9.1 constraint applies — Dhiraj confirms ≥90-day-post-bereavement criterion + voluntary-participation + stop-anytime-right at introduction time; confirmation recorded in `trustee-review-log` pre-conversation-approval row.

3. **Q9.3 — Reviewer-of-record = BOTH trustees panel-wide.** Recommended given Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation downstream impact on UX spec §12 + Epic 6 (claim filing) + Epic 9 (NomineeConsole Sunita's surface) + Epic 11b (memorial) design freezes. Both trustees co-review synthesis at `_bmad-output/research/p0-2b-bereaved-spouse.md`; both sign off; Decision 2026-05-31-009 supersession authored as joint sign-off entry.

4. **Q9.1 constraint operationalization.** The 90-day-post-bereavement + voluntary + stop-anytime safeguard is recorded as a load-bearing structural property of the recruitment + conversation procedure. Operations: (a) Dhiraj's introduction confirms ≥90-day elapse from bereavement event; (b) introductory communication explicitly names voluntary participation with no consequence for declining; (c) opening conversational protocol restates the right to stop at any time without explanation or rationale required; (d) any indicator of distress triggers immediate conversation suspension per ethics-protocol §3. Recording in `host-engagement-log` per-event columns.

5. **Pattern 4 evaluation cross-coupling.** Synthesis Pattern 4 dignified-validation grammar evaluation per AC-1 + AC-2 outcome drives sample-copy revisions in UX spec §12 BEFORE Epic 6 design freeze; coordination with PRD-edit discipline per `[[feedback_architecture_vs_prd_boundary]]`.

**Open follow-ups:**

- Dhiraj Rahul facilitates TSCT-network introduction within 30 days of this Decision; introduction event logged in `trustee-review-log` pre-conversation-approval row with 90-day-criterion confirmation + voluntary-participation acknowledgment.
- Solo Builder conducts ≥60-min Hindi conversation at spouse's chosen setting per Task 8.
- Tasks 9-11 synthesis + joint trustee review + Pattern 4 revisions integrated into UX spec §12 before Epic 6 + Epic 9 + Epic 11b design freezes.
- If Dhiraj's TSCT path yields no candidate within 30 days: supersession entry expanding Q9.2 to Kalpana's network or joint facilitation.
